  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物范围扩展中的彩色区域不仅包含谱系存活计数信息，还编码了更复杂的动态信息。需要从单一的终点图像中恢复出整个扩展过程的边界流动历史，并测试视觉模式是否与传递性标量适应度层次结构兼容。

Method: 1. 提出计算机视觉逆问题：从多类型扩展的终点图像恢复半径索引的成对边界流场
2. 从对数极坐标中的扇区边界曲线提取几何信号
3. 开发理论分析：端点可观测性、稳定性分析、传递/循环分解、接触完全圆形设计
4. 建立确定性基准：解析端点图像、模糊/噪声像素往返测试、标量零应力测试
5. 实现非学习的冻结前沿模拟器，从端点图像恢复成对边缘流历史

Result: 1. 证明了端点可观测性和冻结前沿的稳定性
2. 实现了从端点图像成功恢复成对边缘流历史
3. 在机制性四类型扩展中检测到循环残差
4. 将循环残差用作无维主动设计控制层的强迫信号，覆盖反应扩散控制、表型前沿优化、协议合成等应用

Conclusion: 微生物范围扩展的彩色扇区编码了超越谱系计数的丰富信息，通过计算机视觉方法可以从单一终点图像恢复动态历史。该方法不仅能检测适应度层次结构，还能利用残差信息进行主动控制设计，为微生物扩展工程和种群状态分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Colored sectors in a microbial range expansion encode more than lineage survival counts. We formulate a computer-vision inverse problem: from one endpoint image of an accretive multi-type expansion, recover the radius-indexed pairwise boundary-flow field and test whether the visual pattern is compatible with a transitive scalar fitness hierarchy. The observable is a geometric signal extracted from sector-boundary curves in log-polar coordinates. We prove endpoint observability and stability for frozen fronts, weighted transitive/cyclic decomposition, contact-complete circular design, physical-clock and mechanism non-identifiability, exact Gaussian cyclicity testing, and Bonferroni-valid interval scanning. The benchmark is deterministic: analytic endpoint images, blurred/noisy pixel round trips, scalar-null stress tests, public-image tracing, multi-resolution mechanistic endpoints, and a non-learning frozen-front simulator. The implementation recovers pairwise edge-flow histories from endpoint images, detects cyclic residuals in a mechanistic four-type expansion, and uses those residuals as forcing signals for a dimensionless active design-control layer covering reaction-diffusion control, phenotype-frontier optimization, protocol synthesis, Monte Carlo robustness, and a downstream population-state bridge.

</details>


### [38] [Vitality-Aware Compression for Efficient Image-to-Shape Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00382)
*Jaeah Lee,Hyunjin Kim,Jaewoong Cho,Gihyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了首个针对图像到形状扩散变换器(DiTs)的压缩方法，可在保持几何保真度的同时显著减小模型尺寸


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D形状生成取得显著进展，但大型基于DiT的模型在资源受限环境中计算成本过高，且现有扩散模型压缩策略难以直接迁移到3D生成领域，先前3D效率方法主要关注推理速度而非主干网络压缩

Method: 建立针对图像到形状DiTs的几何感知压缩框架，基于观察到3D DiT层对几何合成具有非均匀重要性，引入了活力引导框架，整合结构化剪枝、自适应量化和针对性微调

Result: 方法实现了在保持合成保真度与全尺寸模型相当的同时，跨最先进图像到3D模型的模型尺寸最多减少66%

Conclusion: 该框架作为即插即用解决方案，在各种模型中展示了高效3D形状生成的潜力，为资源受限环境提供了可行的压缩方案

Abstract: We propose the first compression approach for image-to-shape Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) that substantially reduces model size while preserving geometric fidelity. Despite remarkable progress in 3D shape generation, large DiT-based models remain computationally prohibitive in resource-constrained settings. Furthermore, it is difficult to directly transfer existing diffusion model compression strategies developed for different domains to 3D generation, and prior 3D efficiency approaches focus primarily on inference speed rather than backbone compression. To address this limitation, we build a geometry-aware compression framework tailored to image-to-shape DiTs. Guided by the observation that 3D DiT layers exhibit non-uniform importance for geometry synthesis, we introduce a vitality-guided framework integrating structured pruning, adaptive quantization, and targeted fine-tuning. Our method achieves up to 66% model-size reduction across state-of-the-art image-to-3D models while maintaining synthesis fidelity comparable to full-sized counterparts. This highlights the potential of our framework as a plug-and-play solution for efficient 3D shape generation across diverse models.

</details>


### [39] [DriveVer: Lightweight Trajectory Evaluator as Test-Time Verifier for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00399)
*Chong He,Yuechen Luo,Fang Li,Shaoqing Xu,Fuxi Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveVer：一个轻量级、即插即用的测试时验证器，用于自动驾驶轨迹验证与精炼，通过测试时扩展范式提升基础规划模型的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型存在性能瓶颈，训练时扩展导致高计算成本和边际收益递减。现有规划器采用一次性生成范式，缺乏二次验证和主动修正机制来检测和修正次优或不安全的轨迹

Method: 提出DriveVer验证器，构建基于NAVSIM基准的专用轨迹数据集，采用条件驱动聚类和平衡采样策略。采用双头架构，有效融合候选轨迹与多视图视觉表示和自车运动学特征，同时预测安全置信度得分和绝对几何精炼向量

Result: 在NAVSIM基准上的广泛实验表明，DriveVer显著提升基础规划模型的性能。仅34M参数的紧凑模型引入最小计算开销，实现竞争性结果的同时保持实时推理效率

Conclusion: DriveVer通过测试时扩展范式为自动驾驶系统提供轻量级轨迹验证与精炼能力，解决了传统一次性生成规划器缺乏验证机制的问题，在保持实时性能的同时显著提升规划安全性

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models often encounter performance bottlenecks, as training-time scaling leads to high computational costs and diminishing marginal returns. Existing planners typically adopt a one-shot generation paradigm, lacking secondary validation and active correction mechanisms to detect and revise suboptimal or unsafe trajectories during inference. To address this issue, we propose DriveVer, a lightweight, plug-and-play Test-Time Verifier that leverages the test-time scaling paradigm to enable autonomous driving systems to validate and refine trajectories without costly and heavy training. We construct a dedicated trajectory dataset based on the NAVSIM benchmark through condition-driven clustering and balanced sampling according to ego-vehicle states and navigation commands. Employing a dual-head architecture, DriveVer efficiently fuses candidate trajectories with multi-view visual representations and ego-vehicle kinematic features to simultaneously predict a safety confidence score and an absolute geometric refinement vector. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that DriveVer significantly improves the performance of base planning models. Notably, as an extremely compact model with only 34M parameters, DriveVer introduces minimal computational overhead, achieving competitive results while maintaining real-time inference efficiency.

</details>


### [40] [The Illusion of High Utility in Safety Alignment of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00402)
*Adeel Yousaf,Soumik Ghosh,James Beetham,Amrit Singh Bedi,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现现有文本到图像扩散模型的安全对齐方法虽然表面上有高安全性高实用性，但实际上通过结构化评估发现语义保真度显著下降，根源是文本编码器嵌入空间的语义塌缩现象，作者提出了结构感知几何正则化方法来解决这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型的安全对齐方法评估过于依赖粗粒度的全局效用指标（如FID、CLIPScore），这些指标对细粒度语义正确性不敏感，导致产生了高实用性的假象。需要通过结构化评估来揭示真实的语义保真度损失问题。

Method: 1. 使用TIFA（文本到图像忠实度评估与问答）进行结构化评估，揭示安全对齐模型在对象计数、属性和关系方面的语义保真度下降
2. 分析文本编码器提示嵌入空间，发现语义塌缩现象：嵌入分布收缩和提示间相似性结构扭曲
3. 提出结构感知几何正则化（SAGE），通过明确保留嵌入分布和提示间关系结构的安全对齐目标来解决语义塌缩问题

Result: 1. 传统安全对齐方法在TIFA评估中语义保真度显著下降
2. 语义塌缩现象与结构化效用损失强烈相关
3. SAGE方法相比先前最优方法在TIFA指标上提升5.0%，同时保持强安全性能和竞争力的粗粒度效用分数

Conclusion: 当前文本到图像扩散模型的安全对齐方法存在语义塌缩问题，导致细粒度语义保真度下降。提出的SAGE方法通过几何正则化有效解决了这一问题，在保持安全性的同时显著提升了结构化效用，为安全对齐方法的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Safety alignment of text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models aims to suppress harmful generations while preserving utility on benign prompts. Recent methods often appear to deliver high safety with high utility, but this conclusion rests largely on coarse global utility metrics (e.g., FID, CLIPScore) that are insensitive to fine-grained semantic correctness, creating an illusion of high utility. We show that when utility is measured with structured evaluation, this illusion breaks: on TIFA (Text-to-Image Faithfulness evaluation with Question Answering), safety-aligned models suffer substantial drops in semantic fidelity, including failures in object counts, attributes, and relationships. To diagnose the source of this gap, we analyze the text-encoder prompt embedding space and uncover semantic collapse, a contraction of embedding spread coupled with distortion of inter-prompt similarity structure, which strongly correlates with structured utility loss. Guided by this insight, we propose StructureAware Geometric Regularization (SAGE), a safety alignment objective that explicitly preserves embedding spread and inter-prompt relational structure during adaptation. Our method restores structured utility (TIFA +5.0% over prior state-of-the-art) while maintaining strong safety performance and competitive coarse-grained utility scores. Our source code and trained models are available at https://adeelyousaf.github.io/SAGE_ECCV26_Project_Page/.

</details>


### [41] [MedCAGD: Context-Aware Gated Decoder for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00409)
*Saad Wazir,Patrick Dominique Vibild,Dinh Phu Tran,Seongah Kim,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于医学图像分割的上下文感知门控解码器，通过轻量级多尺度通道重校准、门控跳跃融合与空间竞争以及全局上下文聚合机制，显著提升分割准确性，在11个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模预训练和基于transformer的编码器在特征提取方面取得显著进展，但医学图像分割的准确性仍然受限于解码器设计，特别是在跨尺度对齐、上下文集成和边界保留方面。现有方法在将丰富的特征表示转换为准确的像素级预测时面临低对比度、结构模糊性和尺度变化等挑战。

Method: 提出一种从解码器为中心的角度重新审视医学图像分割，设计上下文感知门控解码器。该方法包含三个核心组件：1）轻量级多尺度通道重校准；2）具有空间竞争的门控跳跃融合；3）将编码器全局信息注入中间解码阶段的全局上下文聚合机制。该设计能够有效将强大的预训练编码器表示转换为空间一致的预测。

Result: 在11个医学图像分割基准数据集上进行的广泛实验验证了该方法的有效性。所提出的方法在保持计算实用性的同时，始终优于强基线模型，证明了其在提升分割准确性方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过专注于解码器设计的改进，提出的上下文感知门控解码器能够有效解决医学图像分割中的关键挑战，特别是跨尺度对齐、上下文集成和边界保留问题。该方法为实现准确且计算高效的医学图像分割提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation relies on the ability of encoder-decoder architectures to translate rich feature representations into accurate pixel-level predictions under challenging conditions such as low contrast, structural ambiguity, and scale variability. While recent advances in large-scale pretraining and transformer-based encoders have substantially improved feature extraction, segmentation accuracy remains constrained by decoder design, particularly in terms of cross-scale alignment, contextual integration, and boundary preservation. In this work, we revisit medical image segmentation from a decoder-centric perspective and propose a context-aware gated decoder that systematically regulates feature fusion and contextual aggregation throughout the decoding process. The proposed decoder integrates lightweight multi-scale channel recalibration, gated skip fusion with spatial competition and a global context aggregation mechanism that injects encoder-wide information into intermediate decoding stages. This design enables effective translation of strong pretrained encoder representations into spatially consistent predictions. Extensive experiments across 11 medical image segmentation benchmarks validate the effectiveness and demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms strong baselines while remaining computationally practical. Code: https://github.com/saadwazir/MedCAGD

</details>


### [42] [MindAU: EEG-Conditioned Facial Action Unit Editing via Dual-Stream Manifold Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00410)
*Zhenhang Li,Xin Zhou,Hao Deng,Lijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MindAU是一个从EEG信号控制面部动作单元（AU）编辑的统一框架，通过双流流形对齐和多模态扩散编辑实现身份保持的面部表情编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑解码研究主要关注从神经信号重建外部感知的视觉内容，但使用EEG记录引导面部表情编辑仍是一个未被充分探索的挑战。该研究需要从噪声EEG信号中识别面部动作相关模式，并将其定位到保持身份的面部表情编辑中。

Method: 1) 通过时间掩码重建和AU分类监督学习噪声鲁棒且AU可区分的EEG表示；2) 通过双流流形对齐将EEG特征与AU级文本语义和身份减少的视觉位移轨迹在多模态空间对齐；3) 将EEG感知的多模态旋转位置嵌入、地标引导参考掩码和AU感知区域监督整合到多模态扩散编辑器中。

Result: 提出了E-CAFE基准数据集，包含配对的EEG-面部编辑样本和标准化评估协议。大量实验证明了MindAU的有效性，展示了其作为面向面部神经肌肉障碍患者的辅助表达技术的潜力。

Conclusion: MindAU框架在EEG条件下面部图像编辑方面取得了重要进展，通过噪声鲁棒的EEG表示学习、跨模态对齐和多模态扩散编辑，实现了精细的面部动作单元控制，为未来的辅助表达技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent brain decoding studies have made substantial progress in reconstructing externally perceived visual content from neural signals. However, using electroencephalography (EEG) recordings to guide facial expression editing remains largely unexplored and poses a distinct challenge: rather than recovering what a subject sees, it requires identifying facial-action related patterns from noisy EEG signals and grounding them in localized, identity-preserving expression edits. In this paper, we investigate EEG-conditioned facial image editing for fine-grained facial action unit (AU) control and propose MindAU, a unified framework for controlling facial AU edits from EEG signals. MindAU first learns noise-robust and AU-discriminative EEG representations through temporal masked reconstruction and AU classification supervision. It then bridges the modality gap via Dual-Stream Manifold Alignment, aligning EEG features with AU-level text semantics and identity-reduced visual displacement trajectories in the multimodal space of Qwen2.5-VL. Finally, MindAU incorporates EEG-aware Multimodal Rotary Positional Embeddings, landmark-guided reference masking, and AU-aware region supervision into a multimodal diffusion-based editor for high-fidelity identity-preserving editing. We also introduce E-CAFE, a curated benchmark for EEG-Conditioned Action-Unit Facial Editing with paired EEG-face editing samples and standardized evaluation protocols. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of MindAU and suggest its potential as a step towards future assistive expression technologies for individuals with facial neuromuscular disorders.

</details>


### [43] [DroneIQA-VLE: Multi-Task Drone Image Quality Assessment via Vision-Language Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00416)
*Wei Sun,Weixia Zhang,Hongjian Zhan,Mingkai Lu,Yixuan Gao,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DroneIQA-VLE是一个用于低空无人机图像目标感知图像质量评估的解决方案，在ICME 2026挑战赛中获得了第二名。它通过集成两种互补的管道来联合预测全局、目标和背景质量分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低空无人机图像的目标感知图像质量评估问题，特别是针对ICME 2026 Drone-IQA Grand Challenge的需求。需要开发一个能够同时评估全局图像质量、目标区域质量和背景质量的综合框架。

Method: 提出一个双管道集成框架：1) 使用SigLIP2视觉编码器配合多任务回归头；2) 采用LoRA适配的Qwen3.5-9B多模态大语言模型进行质量分数回归。两种管道的输出通过算术平均得到最终全局质量预测。

Result: 在ICME 2026 Drone-IQA挑战赛中获得了第二名，证明了该方法的有效性。代码已在GitHub上开源：https://github.com/sunwei925/DroneIQA-VLE。

Conclusion: DroneIQA-VLE框架通过集成传统视觉编码器和多模态大语言模型的优势，有效解决了低空无人机图像的目标感知质量评估问题，在挑战赛中取得了优异的表现。

Abstract: We present DroneIQA-VLE, our solution to the ICME 2026 Drone-IQA Grand Challenge on Target-aware Image Quality Assessment for Low-altitude UAV Images. The framework jointly predicts global, target, and background quality scores by ensembling two complementary pipelines: (1) SigLIP2 vision encoders with multi-task regression heads, and (2) a LoRA-adapted Qwen3.5-9B multimodal large language model for quality score regression. The final global quality prediction is obtained by arithmetically averaging the outputs of both pipelines. Our method achieves 2nd place in the challenge, demonstrating its effectiveness. The code is available at https://github.com/sunwei925/DroneIQA-VLE.

</details>


### [44] [EO-VGGT: Orbital Ray-Conditioned 3D Foundation Models for Satellite Multi-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00417)
*Qiyan Luo,Yingdong Pi,Lekang Wen,Jie Yang,Xiaoyu Wang,Haiming Zhang,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EO-VGGT框架：通过显式物理几何嵌入和优化视图选择，将冻结的透视驱动3D基础模型适配到轨道卫星影像，解决推扫几何与透视假设之间的结构差异问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多视角卫星影像时代，虽然前馈3D基础模型在计算机视觉领域取得了革命性进展，但将其部署到卫星遥感中受到结构差异的制约：隐式透视假设与显式轨道推扫几何之间的不匹配，加上显著的视图集异质性，阻碍了高质量数字表面模型的重建

Method: 1. 几何关联约束选择策略：通过平衡几何多样性和辐射一致性来修剪次优观测数据，优化输入序列
2. 传感器射线编码器：将基于有理函数模型的像素级推扫视线参数化为高维空间几何令牌，弥合中心投影与轨道运动学之间的数学差异
3. 轻量级射线指向感知适配器：使用门控残差块将这些令牌直接集成到冻结的Transformer骨干网络中

Result: 研究表明，将显式物理几何与优化视图选择相结合对于鲁棒的前馈卫星3D重建至关重要。EO-VGGT框架成功地将冻结的透视驱动模型适配到轨道观测

Conclusion: 在卫星星座时代，整合显式物理几何嵌入和优化视图选择是实现高质量地球观测和数字表面模型重建的关键。EO-VGGT框架通过几何关联约束选择、传感器射线编码和射线指向感知适配器，有效解决了轨道遥感中透视假设与推扫几何之间的结构差异问题

Abstract: In the era of satellite constellations, multi-view optical satellite imagery is pivotal for Earth Observation (EO) and high-quality Digital Surface Model (DSM) reconstruction. Although feed-forward 3D foundation models have transformed computer vision, their deployment in satellite remote sensing is inherently constrained by the structural discrepancy between implicit perspective assumptions and explicit orbital pushbroom geometry. This geometric incongruity is further compounded by pronounced view-set heterogeneity. We present EO-VGGT, a framework that adapts a frozen perspective-driven model to orbital observations via explicit physical geometry embedding.First, the Geometry-Correlation Constrained Selection (GCCS) strategy prunes sub-optimal observations by balancing geometric diversity and radiometric consistency to optimize the input sequence. Second, a Sensor-Ray Encoder (SRE) parameterizes pixel-level pushbroom lines of sight derived from the Rational Function Model (RFM) into high-dimensional space-geometric tokens, reconciling the mathematical discrepancy between central projection and orbital kinematics. Third, a lightweight Ray-Pointing-Aware Adapter (RPAA) employs gated residual blocks to integrate these tokens directly into the frozen transformer backbone. Our findings underscore that integrating explicit physical geometry with optimized view selection is essential for robust feed-forward satellite 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [45] [HyFL-CLIP: Hyperbolic Fine-Tuning of CLIP for Robust Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00428)
*Ji Ha Jang,Hayeon Kim,Chulwon Lee,Junghun James Kim,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyFL-CLIP：一个基于双曲空间微调的CLIP框架，通过跨流形相似性蒸馏将欧氏CLIP的知识迁移到双曲空间，以增强对长上下文描述和文本扰动的鲁棒性，特别利用双曲几何捕捉层次和蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CLIP因使用绝对位置编码和短字幕预训练而在处理长上下文描述(>77 tokens)时表现不佳。长上下文中句子常被重新排序、总结或部分省略，先前扩展位置编码的方法在文本扰动下图像-文本对齐性能会下降，这是由于欧氏对比目标强制严格的一对一匹配，缺乏建模全局上下文与其构成元素之间层次关系的显式机制。

Method: 提出HyFL-CLIP：1)通过跨流形相似性蒸馏将已训练的欧氏CLIP知识迁移到双曲空间；2)利用双曲几何捕捉层次和蕴含关系；3)通过链接总结的令牌特征、长上下文描述、构成短文本组件和图像来建模层次语义；4)使用爱因斯坦中点聚合实现双曲蕴含来捕捉部分-整体关系。

Result: 在多个基准测试中表现优异：长上下文跨模态检索、带字幕扰动的跨模态检索、模态内检索和短文本跨模态检索。特别是在文本扰动下，长文本跨模态检索比先前最佳方法提升高达19.5%。该框架可无缝集成到其他模型如Stable Diffusion XL中。

Conclusion: HyFL-CLIP通过将CLIP知识蒸馏到双曲空间，有效地解决了长上下文理解中的挑战，利用双曲几何的自然层次特性增强了模型对文本扰动和复杂语义关系的鲁棒性，为多模态表示学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: CLIP (Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training) has become a de facto paradigm for image-text alignment, but it struggles with long-context descriptions (>77 tokens) due to absolute positional encoding and pretraining on short captions. In long contexts, sentences are often reordered, summarized, or partially omitted. Although prior works extend CLIP with longer positional encodings, they often suffer from degraded image-text alignment under such text perturbations. We attribute this limitation to the Euclidean contrastive objective, which enforces strict one-to-one matching and lacks explicit mechanisms for modeling hierarchical relationships between global context and its constituent elements. To address this issue, we propose HyFL-CLIP, a hyperbolic fine-tuning framework that distills the well-established text-image alignment learned in Euclidean CLIP into hyperbolic space via cross-manifold similarity distillation, leveraging its geometry to capture hierarchical and entailment relations. Our method models hierarchical semantics by linking summarized token-wise features, long-context descriptions, constituent short textual components, and images, capturing part-whole relationships via hyperbolic entailment with Einstein midpoint aggregation. Experiments on diverse benchmarks, including long-context cross-modal retrieval, cross-modal retrieval with caption perturbations, intra-modality retrieval, and short-text cross-modal retrieval, show that HyFL-CLIP achieves more robust long-context understanding. In particular, it yields up to 19.5% improvement in long-text cross-modal retrieval under textual perturbations over the best prior method. We also show HyFL-CLIP can be seamlessly integrated into other model frameworks by applying it to Stable Diffusion XL (SDXL).

</details>


### [46] [Information-Regularized Attention for Visual-Centric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00434)
*Guohao Sun,Xiaofang Wang,Yash Patel,Mengchen Liu,Zhiqiang Tao,Praveen Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出信息正则化注意力（IRA），一种随机注意力机制，用于解决视觉语言模型中存在的物体幻觉、弱视觉接地和全参数指令微调后的灾难性遗忘问题。IRA通过在中间Transformer层注入与视觉信息量相关的局部噪声，显式调节视觉信息注入，从而提升模型的稳定性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型存在三个主要问题：1）物体幻觉，2）弱视觉接地，3）全参数指令微调后的灾难性遗忘。作者认为这些问题的根源在于标准的下一个词预测目标缺乏对视觉表示学习的显式控制，导致视觉嵌入被动优化，容易注入冗余或虚假信号。

Method: 提出信息正则化注意力（IRA），这是一种随机注意力机制，在中间Transformer层的隐藏状态中显式调节视觉信息的注入量。该方法通过局部重参数化将视觉表示的不确定性转化为跨数据点独立的局部噪声。IRA不仅作为正则化器，还作为表示学习的关键组成部分。

Result: IRA在模型性能评估和嵌入属性量化方面均表现出色。它产生更平滑的曲率轨迹，并抑制所有层的注意力汇聚现象，表明视觉信号的转换更加稳定。实验结果表明随机注意力不仅是正则化器，而且是生成架构中表示学习的关键贡献因素。

Conclusion: 信息正则化注意力为构建更可靠的视觉语言模型提供了新的方向。通过显式控制视觉信息的注入，IRA能够解决视觉语言模型中存在的核心问题，并促进更稳定的表示学习过程。该方法将不确定性转化为优势，为多模态学习提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a paradigm for multimodal learning, yet remain unstable due to object hallucination, weak visual grounding, and catastrophic forgetting after full-parameter instruction tuning. We claim these failures result from a lack of explicit control over visual representation learning during the standard next-token prediction objective. As a result, visual embeddings thus become passively optimized and prone to injecting redundant or spurious signals. To counter this, we introduce Information-Regularized Attention (IRA), a stochastic attention mechanism that explicitly regulates the amount of visual information injected into the hidden states of intermediate transformer layers. This local reparameterization translates uncertainty about visual representations into local noise that is independent across data points. Beyond evaluating model performance, we also quantify embedding properties, where IRA produces smoother curvature trajectories and suppresses attention-sink across all layers, indicating a more stable transformation of the visual signal. Our results suggest that stochastic attention is not merely a regularizer but a key contributor to representation learning in a generative architecture, offering a new direction for building more reliable VLMs.

</details>


### [47] [VideoSearch-R1: Iterative Video Retrieval and Reasoning via Soft Query Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00446)
*Seohyun Lee,Seoung Choi,Dohwan Ko,Jongha Kim,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoSearch-R1：一个用于迭代视频检索和推理的智能体框架，通过软查询优化在潜在空间中细化搜索，实现了跨视频检索和视频内时序定位的协同优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两大局限：1）视频检索通常作为预处理步骤，当初始检索失败时缺乏细化机制，导致后续精细推理失败；2）现有智能体框架通常假设相关视频已给定，仅关注视频内推理任务。需要能够同时处理跨视频检索和视频内推理的集成解决方案。

Method: 提出VideoSearch-R1框架，核心创新包括：1）软查询优化(SQR)，在连续潜在空间中细化搜索查询标记而非离散文本空间改写，实现更高效、细粒度调整；2）使用群体相对策略优化(GRPO)训练SQR和推理过程，基于检索和下游任务的任务级奖励信号进行指导

Result: 在三个视频语料库时刻检索(VCMR)数据集上达到最先进性能，能够迭代地从大规模语料库中检索视频、细化搜索查询，并在检索内容中执行精确的查询条件时序定位。分析显示SQR能有效细化原始查询，所需生成标记显著少于显式文本级查询细化

Conclusion: VideoSearch-R1通过集成软查询优化和群体相对策略优化，实现了视频检索和推理的迭代协同，克服了传统方法中检索作为预处理步骤的局限性，为大规模视频理解任务提供了更鲁棒的解决方案

Abstract: As video corpora continue to expand in both scale and task complexity, there is increasing demand for approaches that retrieve relevant videos from large-scale corpora (inter-video reasoning) and subsequently perform fine-grained, query-conditioned tasks (intra-video reasoning) within the retrieved content, such as temporal grounding. However, existing approaches typically treat retrieval as a preprocessing step, and consequently, when the initial retrieval fails, there is no mechanism to refine the search, leading to the failure of subsequent fine-grained intra-video reasoning. Moreover, while recent agentic frameworks have advanced video understanding, they typically assume that the query-relevant video is already given, focusing exclusively on intra-video reasoning tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VideoSearch-R1, an agentic framework for iterative video retrieval and reasoning through multi-turn interaction with a video search engine. Specifically, we introduce Soft Query Refinement (SQR) to refine search query tokens in a continuous latent space rather than rewriting queries in the discrete text space, enabling more efficient and fine-grained adjustments. SQR and its reasoning process are trained using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), guided by task-level reward signals derived from retrieval and downstream tasks. Building upon this, VideoSearch-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance across three datasets on Video Corpus Moment Retrieval (VCMR), iteratively retrieving videos from large-scale corpora, refining search queries, and performing precise query-conditioned temporal grounding within the retrieved content. Our analyses show that SQR effectively refines the original query, requiring significantly fewer generated tokens than explicit text-level query refinement. Code and model checkpoints are publicly available at mlvlab.github.io/VideoSearch-R1.

</details>


### [48] [Multimodal Continuous Reasoning via Asymmetric Mutual Variational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00461)
*Shijie Li,Yilin Gao,Siyuan Yang,Tieyuan Chen,Chaofan Gan,Zhihao He,Zicheng Zhao,Yuyu Guo,Weiyao Lin,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AMVL框架通过双向KL校准解决多模态大语言模型中连续潜变量推理的训练-推理失配问题，防止答案泄漏并提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型存在语言空间瓶颈，将复杂视觉推理压缩为离散标记会丢失感知细节。连续潜变量推理是替代方案，但引入训练-推理失配：训练时基于真实答案的后验会利用答案相关的捷径，导致推理时先验模仿那些测试时无法获得信息的后验，造成性能下降

Method: 提出非对称互变分学习框架，采用双向校准目标：前向KL散度训练与目标无关的先验匹配后验，同时新颖的反向KL散度正则化后验，防止其坍缩到推理不兼容区域，缓解答案泄漏问题

Result: 在潜变量集成MLLM中实例化AMVL，相比强离散和潜变量推理基线持续表现更优：在复杂BLINK基准上平均得分提升+10.83，在个别推理任务上增益高达+32.00，分析确认潜空间稳定性改善

Conclusion: AMVL通过理论分析将泄漏形式化为先验污染，证明双KL目标能减少该问题，为连续潜变量多模态推理提供了有效的训练框架，解决了训练-推理失配的核心挑战

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are often constrained by a language-space bottleneck, forcing complex visual reasoning into discrete tokens which can lose perceptual nuance. A promising alternative is continuous latent reasoning, where the goal is to discover implicit reasoning pathways that bridge the multimodal query and the final answer. However, this introduces a severe train-inference mismatch: a training-time posterior, conditioned on the ground-truth answer, can exploit answer-dependent shortcuts. Standard variational training then forces the inference-time prior to mimic a posterior that has access to information unavailable at test time, leading to poor performance. To address this, we propose Asymmetric Mutual Variational Learning (AMVL), a framework that resolves this mismatch via a bidirectional calibration objective. A forward KL divergence trains the target-agnostic prior to match the posterior, while a novel reverse KL divergence simultaneously regularizes the posterior, preventing it from collapsing into inference-incompatible regions and mitigating this ``answer leakage''. We provide theoretical analysis formalizing this leakage as prior contamination and prove that our dual-KL objective reduces it. We instantiate AMVL in a latent-integrated MLLM and show that it consistently outperforms strong discrete and latent-reasoning baselines, improving the average score on the complex BLINK benchmark by +10.83 and achieving gains of up to +32.00 on individual reasoning tasks, with analyses confirming improved latent-space stability.

</details>


### [49] [StochasT: Learning with Stochastic Turn Depth for Visual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00465)
*Yuan Qing,Chengzhi Mao,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StochasT方法通过随机分组图像的多轮语言任务进行训练，解决大型视觉语言模型在多轮训练和单轮测试之间的不一致问题，提升模型在各种情境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型(LVLMs)训练与评估存在不一致：视觉指令微调(VIT)通常使用同一图像的多个语言任务进行多轮对话训练，而现有基准却在孤立的单轮场景下评估模型。这种不一致导致模型在训练中可能遭受视觉注意力衰减和上下文过拟合，难以在测试阶段发挥全部潜力。

Method: 提出StochasT（随机轮次深度）方法：随机将同一图像的语言任务按有机顺序分组为不同大小的簇（轮次深度），最大化训练数据效用而不丢弃任何数据。同时引入基于平衡拉丁方的基准无关评估机制，测量模型在不同上下文依赖下的鲁棒性。

Result: 大量实验证明，StochasT能有效赋予大型视觉语言模型强大且协调的单轮和多轮使用能力。该方法解决了训练与评估场景不一致的问题，提升了模型在各种情境下的性能表现。

Conclusion: StochasT通过随机分组训练策略解决了大型视觉语言模型训练与评估不一致的核心问题，配合新的评估机制，使模型能够在不同上下文依赖下保持鲁棒性，为实际多轮对话应用提供了更实用的模型能力。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) rely extensively on Visual Instruction Tuning (VIT) to elicit their multimodal reasoning capabilities. However, we find a discrepancy: VIT often packs multiple language tasks about the same image for conversational, multi-turn training, whereas existing benchmarks evaluate LVLMs in isolated, single-turn scenarios. The models can suffer from visual attention decay and contextual overfitting during multi-turn training, making it hard for them to realize their full potential in the mismatched test phase. To close the gap, we propose learning with Stochastic Turn Depth (StochasT), which stochastically groups language tasks for the same image into clusters of varying sizes (turn depth) while preserving their organic order. Hence, while StochasT draws on Dropout and stochastic depth for ResNets, it does not actually drop anything to maximize the utility of the training data. Furthermore, we introduce a challenging, benchmark-agnostic evaluation mechanism based on the Balanced Latin Square to measure LVLMs' robustness under varying contextual dependencies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StochasT effectively grants LVLMs strong, harmonized capabilities for both single-turn and multi-turn use cases.

</details>


### [50] [MindEdit-Bench: Benchmarking Object-Level Counterfactual Spatial Reasoning in VLMs from In-the-Wild Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00491)
*Leyuan Yu,Xiao Tang,Minghao Liu,Xinyuan Li,Xiaokai Bai,Sheng Zhou,Qunshu Lin,Weihao Xuan,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MindEdit-Bench是一个专门评估视觉语言模型(VLMs)空间编辑推理能力的基准测试，包含6个从真实室内场景构建的任务，重点测试物体移动或旋转等反事实推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型基准主要测试观测性空间推理（描述输入中已存在的关系），缺乏对反事实场景推理能力的评估——即预测物体被移动或旋转后的结果。

Method: 1. 从120个新捕获的室内场景构建三张照片的智能手机图像三元组
2. 使用自动的野外3D场景图提取流程
3. 设计6个空间推理任务：4个测试感知和视角变换，2个新任务（L4空间编辑和L5跨视图可见性编辑）测试物体级反事实推理
4. 每个问题提供8-24个结构化答案选择，支持答案字母级别的错误诊断
5. 在15个VLM上进行1,003个人工验证问题的评估

Result: 1. 所有任务中VLM平均准确率仅为8%-31%，而人类多数投票准确率达81%-97%
2. 人类与最佳VLM之间的总体差距为53个百分点，每个任务至少差距39个百分点
3. 结构化答案空间揭示了非均匀失败模式，包括相机深度轴推断较弱以及在困难的可见性编辑案例中的回退行为
4. 基准测试使用了未从公共数据集中提取的120个私人室内场景，减少了预训练数据重叠风险

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在空间编辑和反事实推理方面存在显著缺陷，无法有效预测物体移动或旋转后的结果，与人类性能差距巨大，表明需要更先进的3D空间理解能力来支持实际应用。

Abstract: Benchmarks for vision-language models (VLMs) mostly test observational spatial reasoning: models describe relations already visible in the input. Existing what-if tasks typically vary the observer while keeping the scene fixed. Can VLMs instead predict the consequences of hypothetically moving or rotating an object? We introduce MindEdit-Bench, a benchmark of six spatial reasoning tasks built from three-photo smartphone triplets of newly captured indoor scenes via an automatic in-the-wild 3D scene-graph extraction pipeline. Four tasks probe perception and perspective transformation over observed structure; two new tasks, L4 (spatial editing) and L5 (cross-view visibility editing), probe object-level counterfactual reasoning, where correct answers are absent from all input images. Each question provides 8-24 structured answer choices, enabling answer-letter-level diagnosis of spatial and fallback errors. The benchmark covers 120 private indoor scenes not drawn from public datasets, reducing public-data pretraining-overlap risk. Across 15 VLMs on 1,003 human-verified questions, task-wise mean VLM accuracy is only 8%-31%, versus 81%-97% human majority-vote accuracy. The pooled human--best-VLM gap is 53 pp, with at least 39 pp on every task. The structured answer space further reveals non-uniform failures, including weaker camera-depth-axis inference and fallback behavior on difficult visibility-editing cases.

</details>


### [51] [GenSP: Consistent Spherical Parameterization via Learning Shape Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00492)
*Sai Karthikey Pentapati,Shashank Gupta,Rajesh Sureddi,Yuezhi Yang,Alan C. Bovik,Qixing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenSP是一个数据驱动的框架，通过学习神经生成模型来预测从单位球体到形状数据集中形状的连续映射，从而获得跨零亏格形状集合的一致球面参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常独立优化每个形状的参数化，缺乏跨形状的一致性。作者希望开发一种能够学习跨形状集合的一致球面参数化方法，使相似形状共享一致的参数化表示。

Method: 方法包括：1）引入连续神经变形模型，从球体坐标和潜在形状代码预测表面点，避免网格离散化伪影；2）在训练空间中添加连接球体与输入形状的中间形状，使模型能够学习跨异质形状集合的有意义变形；3）通过在潜在空间中沿训练形状的生成树传播映射来计算可靠的初始对应关系。

Result: 在ShapeNet数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的球面参数化方法相比，该方法显著减少了几何失真并提高了跨形状的一致性。

Conclusion: GenSP通过学习神经生成模型来实现一致球面参数化，该方法避免了独立优化每个形状，通过连续映射和潜在空间传播实现了更好的几何保真度和跨形状一致性，为形状集合的参数化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce GenSP, a data-driven framework that learns consistent spherical parameterizations across a collection of genus-0 shapes. Instead of optimizing the parameterization of each shape independently, our method learns a neural generative model that predicts a continuous mapping from the unit sphere to shapes in a dataset. Under this formulation, spherical parameterizations are obtained through the inverse mappings of the learned generator, which encourages similar shapes to share consistent parameterizations. To make this formulation practical, we address several key challenges in learning such a generative model. First, we introduce a continuous neural deformation model that predicts surface points from sphere coordinates and latent shape codes, avoiding discretization artifacts common in mesh-based formulations. Second, we augment the training space with intermediate shapes that bridge the sphere and input shapes, allowing the model to learn meaningful deformations across a heterogeneous shape collection. Third, we compute reliable initial correspondences by propagating mappings along a spanning tree of training shapes in the latent space. Experiments on the ShapeNet dataset demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces geometric distortion and improves cross-shape consistency compared with state-of-the-art spherical parameterization methods.

</details>


### [52] [HieDG: A Hierarchical Discrete Geometry-Guided Framework for Multi-Animal Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00494)
*Chenxun Deng,Zhongde Zhang,Ye Yuan,Chengyang Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Bohao Chen,Hongying Yan,Hang Zhou,Hua Han,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HieDG是一个基于分层离散几何指导的多动物跟踪框架，通过将连续几何信号转换为稳定的离散令牌来提升身份关联性能，在多个基准测试中实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多动物跟踪面临挑战，包括统一外观、高密度和不规则运动。现有方法通常基于启发式或查询范式：前者依赖手工几何关联而缺乏端到端优化；后者支持联合优化但过度依赖外观嵌入。连续几何嵌入在这些条件下可能不稳定，小的坐标扰动会不成比例地改变跨帧注意力权重，从而降低身份关联性能。

Method: 提出HieDG框架，将几何动态重新表述为查询式跟踪器中的结构化离散表示。使用两阶段残差码本来离散化位置、尺度和速度线索，将不稳定的连续几何转换为结构化、稳定的离散令牌。这些令牌与视觉嵌入对齐并集成到跟踪查询中以增强身份一致性。

Result: 在动物特定基准测试(AnimalTrack、BFT和BuckTales)上表现出最先进的关联性能，HOTA、AssA和IDF1指标有显著提升。在通用多目标跟踪基准(DanceTrack和SportsMOT)上的额外评估也显示出具有竞争力的性能，表明离散几何建模在动物特定场景之外具有更广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过将连续几何信号转换为结构化离散表示，HieDG有效地解决了多动物跟踪中的身份关联问题，为处理统一外观、高密度和不规则运动场景提供了更稳定的解决方案，并在多个基准测试中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Multi-animal tracking (MAT) is critical for wildlife monitoring and behavioral analysis, yet remains challenging due to uniform appearance, high density, and irregular motion. Existing methods typically follow heuristic- or query-based paradigms: the former relies on handcrafted geometric associations without end-to-end optimization, whereas the latter enables joint optimization but relies heavily on appearance embeddings. In such conditions, continuous geometric embeddings can be unstable, as small coordinate perturbations may disproportionately alter cross-frame attention weights, degrading identity association performance. To address this limitation, we propose HieDG, a Hierarchical Discrete Geometry-guided tracking framework that reformulates geometric dynamics as structured discrete representations within a query-based tracker. Instead of directly using raw geometric signals, HieDG employs a two-stage residual codebook to discretize position, scale, and velocity cues, transforming unstable continuous geometry into structured, stable discrete tokens. These tokens are aligned with visual embeddings and integrated into the tracking queries to enhance identity consistency. Extensive experiments on animal-specific benchmarks (AnimalTrack, BFT, and BuckTales) demonstrate state-of-the-art association performance with significant improvements in HOTA, AssA, and IDF1. Additional evaluations on generic multi-object tracking benchmarks, including DanceTrack and SportsMOT, show competitive performance, indicating the broader applicability of discretized geometric modeling beyond animal-specific scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [Robust 3D Alignment of Generative Reconstructions via Partial Monocular Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00498)
*Yuchen Zhang,Luanyuan Dai,Yiwei Wang,Xiwei Xu,Jianing Zhang,Johnny. r. zhang,Xianhui Meng,Yanbiao Ma,Jiayi Ma,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练、可解释的几何对齐框架，解决生成式3D先验与单目部分观测对齐的难题，通过Sim(3)变换恢复精确度量尺度和位姿


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式3D重建与部分单目观测的对齐是计算机视觉中关键但尚未充分探索的挑战。该任务本质上不适定，因为噪声稀疏的单目输入与密集生成先验之间存在严重不对称性，尺度模糊、几何幻觉以及初始缺乏重叠使得传统配准流程失效。

Method: 提出无需训练的几何对齐框架：1) 引入3D相似变换(Sim(3))解决尺度模糊，包含显式尺度因子；2) 采用由粗到细对齐策略，使用几何感知描述子进行鲁棒初始化；3) 解耦的闭式求解器进行精细优化；4) 提出幻觉过滤操作抑制幻觉几何产生的异常值

Result: 方法实现了稳定准确的配准，显著优于经典几何流程和最先进的基于学习的基线方法。同时提出了GenPMOAlign-Where2Place基准测试，专门用于评估生成式与部分单目观测对齐性能

Conclusion: 提出的训练无关、可解释的几何对齐框架有效解决了生成式3D先验与部分单目观测的对齐问题，通过Sim(3)变换、由粗到细策略和幻觉过滤操作，成功克服了尺度模糊、几何幻觉和初始不重叠等挑战

Abstract: Aligning generative 3D reconstructions with partial monocular observations is a critical but under-explored challenge in computer vision. This task is inherently ill-posed due to severe asymmetries between noisy, sparse monocular inputs and dense generative priors, whose scale ambiguity and geometric hallucinations, combined with the lack of initial overlap, render traditional registration pipelines ineffective. To resolve these issues, we propose a training-free and interpretable geometric alignment framework that grounds generative 3D priors via a 3D similarity transformation (Sim(3)), which can recover accurate metric scale and pose. Specifically, we introduce an explicit scale factor to resolve metric ambiguity and employ a coarse-to-fine alignment strategy, leveraging geometry-aware descriptors for robust initialization and a decoupled closed-form solver for precision refinement. In addition, we introduce a Hallucination Filtering operation to effectively suppress outliers caused by hallucinated geometry. To evaluate alignment performance under these extreme conditions, we introduce GenPMOAlign--Where2Place, a rigorous benchmark specifically designed for Generative-to-Partial Monocular Observational Alignment. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves stable and accurate registration, substantially outperforming both classical geometric pipelines and state-of-the-art learning-based baselines. Code and the benchmark will be publicly released.

</details>


### [54] [Cross4D-JEPA: Dense Cross-modal Correspondence Distillation for 4D Point Cloud Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00514)
*Trung Thanh Nguyen,Hai Nguyen-Truong,Tu Vo,Hoang M. Truong,Tuan-Anh Vu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Cross4D-JEPA方法，通过密集跨模态对应将2D基础模型知识蒸馏到4D点编码器中，在多个4D点云基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D点云（3D点序列）在机器人学和具身感知中很重要，但密集标注成本高，自监督预训练是关键。现有方法多为单模态方法，少数跨模态方法仅使用全局嵌入，忽略了2D基础模型中丰富的每块语义信息。

Method: 提出教师-学生方法Cross4D-JEPA：1）密集跨模态对应：将每个3D点映射到教师模型（DINOv2或V-JEPA 2）的对应图像块特征；2）逐点目标：训练学生编码器在潜在空间匹配这些特征，无需掩码、负样本或解码器。

Result: 在MSR-Action3D、DeformingThings4D、NTU-RGB+D 60和HOI4D四个基准上，该方法始终优于单模态和全局跨模态基线，并与更重的已发表4D方法竞争。分析表明提升主要来自对应关系的粒度而非教师模态。此外，所学密集表示具有跨域迁移性，提高标签效率，且在相同训练预算下改善全标签微调性能，13倍小的编码器能与重量级池化主干匹配。

Conclusion: Cross4D-JEPA通过利用2D基础模型的密集每块语义特征，为4D点云学习提供了有效的跨模态知识蒸馏框架，在多个任务上实现了优越性能，展示了密集表示的优势。

Abstract: Automatic understanding of dynamic 4D point clouds, the 3D-point sequences captured over time by depth sensors and LiDAR, is central to robotics and embodied perception. Yet annotating them densely is expensive, making self-supervised pretraining the natural route to transferable representations. Existing pretext tasks, however, are almost entirely intra-modal, and the few methods that transfer knowledge from 2D foundation models rely on a single global embedding per clip, discarding the rich per-patch semantics that these models compute. To address this gap, we propose Cross4D-JEPA, a teacher-student method that distills a frozen 2D foundation model, an image model DINOv2, or a video model V-JEPA 2, into a 4D point encoder. The proposed method combines (1) a dense cross-modal correspondence that maps every 3D point to the teacher patch feature it projects to, and (2) a per-point objective that trains the student to match these features in latent space with no masking, negatives, or decoder. We evaluate Cross4D-JEPA on four benchmarks, MSR-Action3D, DeformingThings4D, NTU-RGB+D 60, and HOI4D, against intra-modal and global cross-modal baselines. Experimental results show that, under a matched protocol, the proposed method consistently outperforms intra-modal and global cross-modal baselines across the four benchmarks and is competitive with heavier published 4D methods; further analysis attributes this gain primarily to the granularity of the correspondence rather than the teacher modality. Beyond recognition accuracy, the dense representation learned by Cross4D-JEPA transfers across domains, improves label efficiency, and improves full-label fine-tuning under the same training budget, while a 13x smaller encoder matches a heavyweight pooling backbone.

</details>


### [55] [Prior-Anchored Debiasing for Long-Tailed Multi-Organ Pathology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00499)
*Feng Yang,Jie Liu,Yubo Pang,Peilin Chen,Xinheng Lyu,Shiqi Wang,Howard Leung,Ping Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PriOrGen框架解决多器官病理报告生成中的长尾分布问题，通过视觉原型锚定瓶颈模块和元报告锚定库模块来缓解视觉表示和文本解码偏差，在头尾器官类别上都取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的病理报告生成方法主要针对单一器官场景开发，忽视了临床实践中常见的多器官场景。在多器官场景中，器官类型通常遵循长尾分布，现有方法存在两个关键偏差：视觉表示偏差（编码器偏向头类模式而非尾类的判别性特征）和文本解码偏差（解码器对头类叙述模式过拟合，导致尾类器官的诊断输出不可靠）。

Method: 提出了Prior-anchored multi-Organ pathology report Generation框架（PriOrGen）。具体包括：1) 视觉原型锚定瓶颈模块：利用信息瓶颈原理与可学习的锚定表示，选择性保留诊断相关的视觉信息，同时过滤出头类偏向的冗余信息；2) 元报告锚定库模块：构建器官特定的元报告锚定库，检索器官忠实的文本先验，引导解码器远离头类叙述模式。

Result: 在多器官病理数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法能有效缓解长尾偏差，并在头类和尾类器官类别上都实现了比现有最先进方法更优越的报告生成性能。

Conclusion: PriOrGen框架成功解决了多器官病理报告生成中的长尾分布问题，通过创新的视觉和文本偏差缓解策略，为临床实践中真实的多器官场景提供了可靠的自动化病理报告生成解决方案。

Abstract: Automated pathology report generation from Whole Slide Images (WSIs) has
  attracted increasing attention in digital pathology. However, existing methods
  are predominantly developed under single-organ settings, overlooking the
  multi-organ scenarios encountered in clinical practice, where organ types
  typically follow a long-tailed distribution. To address this gap, we identify
  two critical biases: (1) visual representation bias, where the encoder favors
  head-class patterns over tail-class discriminative features, and (2) textual
  decoding bias, where the decoder overfits to head-class narrative patterns,
  yielding diagnostically unreliable outputs for tail-class organs. To mitigate
  these two biases, we propose a novel Prior-anchored multi-Organ pathology
  report Generation framework (PriOrGen). Specifically, a Visual-Prototype
  Anchored Bottleneck module leverages the information bottleneck principle with
  learnable anchor representations to selectively retain diagnostically relevant
  visual information while filtering out head-biased redundancy. Secondly, a
  Meta-Report Anchored Bank module constructs an organ-specific meta-report
  anchored bank and retrieves organ-faithful textual priors to steer the decoder
  away from head-class narrative patterns. Extensive experiments on a multi-
  organ pathology dataset demonstrate that our method effectively mitigates
  long-tail biases and achieves superior report generation performance across
  both head and tail organ categories compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [56] [EgoGapBench: Benchmarking Egocentric Action Selection in Multi-Agent Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00547)
*Jihyeok Jung,Jeewu Lee,Sanghyeop Kim,Chanhee Han,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGapBench是一个诊断性基准测试，用于评估多智能体场景中的自我中心行动选择能力，该基准分离了自我中心视角理解与第一人称视觉理解，发现现有MLLMs在此任务上表现远低于人类水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心基准主要基于第一人称视觉数据构建，难以在孤立情况下评估纯粹的自我中心视角理解能力。理解第一人称视觉输入和采取自我中心视角是可分离的能力，特别是在缺乏第一人称身体线索或其他智能体存在时。需要分离出纯粹的自我中心视角理解能力进行评估。

Method: 引入EgoGapBench诊断基准，用于测量多智能体自我中心场景中的行动选择能力。定义了"自我中心行动选择"能力：在存在其他智能体的情况下，从特定智能体的视角选择合适的行动。对开源和专有MLLMs进行测试，并进行微调实验以评估改进效果。

Result: 在EgoGapBench上，人类回答可靠，而开源和专有MLLMs表现显著较差，系统性地选择其他可见智能体执行的动作。基于现有自我中心数据的微调无法缩小差距，甚至可能有害。基于EgoGapBench训练数据的微调能提高准确性，但仍未达到人类水平。

Conclusion: 自我中心行动选择能力难以仅从第一人称视觉数据中获得。MLLMs不仅需要针对场景理解进行评估和训练，还需要针对自我中心行动选择进行专门训练。这揭示了当前模型在理解多智能体场景中特定视角行动选择方面的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Existing egocentric benchmarks have primarily constructed the egocentric setting from first-person-view data, which makes it difficult to evaluate egocentric perspective itself in isolation. However, understanding first-person-view input and taking an egocentric perspective are separable abilities, especially when first-person body cues are absent or when other agents are present. To isolate egocentric perspective understanding, we introduce EgoGapBench, a diagnostic benchmark for measuring action selection in multi-agent egocentric scenes. We define the ability measured by this benchmark as Egocentric Action Selection (EAS): selecting an appropriate action from the agent's perspective in the presence of other agents. On EgoGapBench, humans answer reliably, whereas both open-source and proprietary MLLMs perform substantially worse and systematically select actions performed by other visible agents. Fine-tuning on existing egocentric data fails to close this gap and can even be detrimental. In contrast, fine-tuning on EgoGapBench training data improves accuracy but does not reach human performance. These results show that EAS is difficult to acquire from first-person-view data alone, and that MLLMs should be evaluated and trained not only for scene understanding but also for egocentric action selection.

</details>


### [57] [AnF-DiffPET: Anatomy- and Frequency-Guided Diffusion for PET/CT Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00509)
*Xuepeng Liu,Ruili Li,Zetong Liu,Renyiming Li,Yan Li,Yin Dai,Chao Li,Yueyang Teng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnF-DiffPET：一种基于CT指导的解剖-频率引导扩散框架，用于低剂量PET去噪，通过整合解剖-频率引导、多尺度交叉Transformer重建和频率对比硬挖掘技术来提升去噪性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低注射活度或缩短采集时间会产生低剂量PET图像，其具有更强的计数依赖噪声和较不可靠的摄取值量化。传统扩散模型在PET去噪中面临解剖指导不足、多尺度特征传播不稳定以及频域摄取值恢复不确定等挑战。

Method: 提出AnF-DiffPET框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 解剖-频率引导(AFG)增强解剖感知特征调制；2) 多尺度交叉Transformer重建(MSCTR)稳定多尺度特征传播；3) 频率对比硬挖掘(FCHM)保持频域一致性。该方法是基于CT条件化的低剂量PET去噪扩散框架。

Result: 在四个PET/CT数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在图像保真度、解剖一致性和量化保真度方面优于代表性的基于CNN、GAN、Transformer和扩散的基线方法。

Conclusion: AnF-DiffPET通过解剖和频率指导有效地解决了低剂量PET去噪的挑战，提高了去噪质量，代码和训练模型将在论文接受后公开。

Abstract: Positron emission tomography (PET) provides essential functional information for disease assessment, however reducing injected activity or acquisition time produces low-dose (LD) PET with stronger count dependent noise and less reliable uptake quantification. Diffusion models offer a promising solution for PET denoising by progressively recovering high-dose (HD) PET images from LD inputs. However, LD-to-HD PET denoising is still challenging due to insufficient anatomical guidance, unstable multi-scale feature propagation, and uncertain frequency domain uptake recovery. We propose AnF-DiffPET, an anatomy- and frequency-guided diffusion framework for computed tomography (CT) conditioned LD PET denoising. The framework integrates Anatomical-Frequency Guidance (AFG), Multi-Scale Cross-Transformer Reconstruction (MSCTR), and Frequency-Contrastive Hard Mining (FCHM) to enhance anatomy aware feature modulation and frequency domain consistency during denoising. Experimental results across four PET/CT datasets show that the proposed method improves image fidelity, anatomical consistency, and quantitative fidelity over representative CNN-based, GAN-based, transformer-based, and diffusion-based methods. The code and trained models will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [58] [Identifying Latent Concepts and Structures for Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00620)
*Boyang Dai,Chaoqi Chen,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CPF-GCD：一种用于广义类别发现的表示学习框架，通过低秩组合原语字段重构特征空间，使潜藏结构可识别，将类别视为有限视觉原语的组合和空间排列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GCD方法主要关注设计聚类目标，但忽略了关键瓶颈：标准视觉骨干网络产生高秩、纠缠的令牌表示，这些表示不适合无监督发现潜在概念和结构。现有方法忽视了特征空间的低秩组合组织这一重要属性。

Method: 提出组合原语字段（CPF-GCD），一种表示学习框架，通过在骨干网络和头部之间插入空间字段机制，强制实施低秩组合组织。该机制通过低秩原语混合重写噪声补丁令牌，将图像分解为可重复使用的原子部分及其空间布局，从而显式建模原语的空间分布。

Result: 大量实验表明，CPF作为一个通用的即插即用模块，在各种GCD基线中持续提升性能。这验证了识别和利用低秩组合结构是开放世界识别的关键归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 通过强制实施低秩组合组织来重塑特征空间，使潜在结构可识别，是GCD中的关键改进。CPF将表示焦点从仅仅分割全局嵌入转移到构建结构化和可分离的原语字段，使新类别能够作为共享词汇上的新激活模式自然出现。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to recognize known classes while autonomously discovering novel ones in open-world settings. However, current approaches primarily focus on designing clustering objectives, often overlooking a critical bottleneck: standard vision backbones yield high-rank, entangled token representations that are ill-suited for unsupervised discovery of latent concepts and structures. In this paper, we propose Compositional Primitive Fields (CPF-GCD), a novel representation learning framework that reshapes the feature space to make such latent structure identifiable by enforcing a low-rank compositional organization. Our core hypothesis is that all categories, whether known or novel, can be expressed as compositions and spatial arrangements of a finite set of learnable visual primitives that capture reusable concepts. CPF instantiates this geometric constraint via a spatial field mechanism. Inserted between the backbone and the head, it rewrites noisy patch tokens through low-rank primitive mixtures, effectively decomposing images into reusable atomic parts and their spatial layouts. By explicitly modeling the spatial distribution of primitives, CPF enables novel categories to emerge naturally as new activation patterns over a shared vocabulary. This shifts the focus of representation from merely partitioning global embeddings to constructing a structured and separable primitive field. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CPF serves as a generic, plug-and-play module that consistently boosts performance across diverse GCD baselines, validating that identifying and leveraging low-rank compositional structure is a crucial inductive bias for open-world recognition.

</details>


### [59] [Restore3D: Breathing Life into Broken Objects with Shape and Texture Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00522)
*Xiaolong Shen,Zongxin Yang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Restore3D是一个同时恢复破损3D物体形状和纹理的新框架，使用多视图图像和自动数据生成管道，通过Mask Self-Perceiver模块和Depth-Aware Mask Rectifier实现高质量修复，在合成和真实破损物体基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恢复不完整或损坏的3D物体对于文化遗产保护、遮挡物体重建和艺术设计至关重要。现有方法主要关注几何补全，常常忽略纹理恢复，并且在处理相对复杂多样的物体时存在困难。

Method: 1. 开发自动数据生成管道，从大规模3D数据集中合成配对的不完整-完整样本以解决训练数据有限问题。2. 引入多视图模型，配备精心设计的Mask Self-Perceiver模块和Depth-Aware Mask Rectifier。3. 使用自感知器学习的修正掩码引导图像集成和增强阶段，保留观测到的形状和纹理模式，同时细化生成区域。4. 采用从粗到精的重建策略，从精化的多视图图像中恢复详细的纹理化3D网格。

Result: 在合成和真实破损物体基准测试中，Restore3D在多视图恢复质量和纹理网格重建方面优于代表性的修复、补全和重建基线方法，能够生成高分辨率、语义一致且视图一致的多视图图像。

Conclusion: Restore3D框架通过同时恢复形状和纹理，解决了现有方法在纹理恢复和处理复杂物体方面的局限性，在文化遗产保护等应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Restoring incomplete or damaged 3D objects is crucial for cultural heritage preservation, occluded object reconstruction, and artistic design. Existing methods primarily focus on geometric completion, often neglecting texture restoration and struggling with relatively complex and diverse objects. We introduce Restore3D, a novel framework that simultaneously restores both the shape and texture of broken objects using multi-view images. To address limited training data, we develop an automated data generation pipeline that synthesizes paired incomplete-complete samples from large-scale 3D datasets. Central to Restore3D is a multi-view model, enhanced by a carefully designed Mask Self-Perceiver module with a Depth-Aware Mask Rectifier. The rectified masks learned by the self-perceiver guide an image integration and enhancement phase, helping retain observed shape and texture patterns while refining the generated regions and mitigating the low-resolution limitations of the base model, yielding high-resolution, semantically coherent, and view-consistent multi-view images. A coarse-to-fine reconstruction strategy is then employed to recover detailed textured 3D meshes from refined multi-view images. Experiments on synthetic and real broken-object benchmarks show that Restore3D improves multi-view restoration quality and textured-mesh reconstruction over representative inpainting, completion, and reconstruction baselines in the evaluated settings. Project Page: restore3dx.github.io

</details>


### [60] [SPECSIA: Stylization Dataset for Novel-View Enhancement in Drawing-based 3D Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00525)
*Kyuwon Kim,Sunjae Yoon,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DraViE模块和SPECSIA-15K数据集，用于提升单张2D手绘图的动画生成质量，解决新视角下的投影伪影问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于手绘的3D动画流程通常使用逐样本2D细化来对齐输入图像，但这种方法容易过度拟合到观察视角，无法修正新视角中的投影伪影

Method: 1. 创建SPECSIA-15K数据集，包含14,980对有伪影的投影/细化目标对；2. 提出DraViE轻量级即插即用模块，利用数据级先验去除新视角伪影，同时保持风格和运动合理性

Result: 实验显示在新视角保真度和时间一致性方面取得一致提升，且每个角色的适应成本低于逐样本微调方法

Conclusion: DraViE模块能有效解决基于单张2D手绘图的动画生成中的新视角伪影问题，相比现有方法具有更好的保真度、时间一致性和成本效率

Abstract: Generating animation from a single 2D drawing is challenging because the output must preserve character appearance while remaining plausible and temporally coherent under motion. Existing drawing-based 3D animation pipelines often use sample-wise 2D refinement to align animated renderings with the input image, but such optimization tends to overfit to the observed view and fails to correct projection-induced artifacts in novel views. To address this limitation, we introduce SPECSIA-15K, a paired stylization dataset containing 14,980 artifact-corrupted projection/refinement-target pairs from 1,498 3DBiCar characters. We further present DraViE (Drawing-based View Enhancement), a lightweight plug-and-play module trained with data-level priors to remove novel-view artifacts while preserving style and motion plausibility. Experiments show consistent gains in novel-view fidelity and temporal coherence with lower per-character adaptation cost than sample-wise fine-tuning.

</details>


### [61] [LUMA: Benchmarking Segmentation via a Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00687)
*Tobias Christian Nauen,Anosh Billimoria,Federico Raue,Stanislav Frolov,Brian B. Moser,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了LUMA（轻量级通用掩码适配器），这是一个与主干网络无关的掩码Transformer头部，可以将任何主干网络作为黑盒特征提取器使用。研究发现普通ViT在分割任务中仍然保持最佳效率平衡，而预训练目标对分割质量的影响比架构更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像分割任务中比较不同Transformer主干网络存在混杂因素：每个主干网络都搭配不同的解码器、训练方案和预训练策略，因此报告的性能差异很少能真正反映主干网络本身的质量。需要一个公平的比较框架来评估主干网络的真实性能。

Method: 提出了LUMA（Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter），一个轻量级、与主干网络无关的掩码Transformer头部。LUMA将任何主干网络作为黑盒特征提取器，通过廉价的交叉注意力机制让一组查询从主干特征中读取信息。使用固定的LUMA头部，在ADE20K和Cityscapes数据集上，对20个主干网络、11种预训练方案和不同分辨率进行了统一基准测试。

Result: 1. LUMA能够匹配最先进的EoMT（高效ViT分割器）的准确性，但成本更低，并能无缝适配各向同性、分层、卷积和混合专家等各种类型的主干网络。2. 研究发现"高效"的token混合器即使在设计所针对的高分辨率下也无法提供效率优势，普通ViT在所有分辨率下都保持着吞吐量的帕累托前沿。3. 预训练目标对分割质量的影响最大，而不是架构设计。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出了一个公平的主干网络比较框架，并揭示了两个重要发现：普通ViT仍然是最有效的分割主干网络，预训练目标比架构设计对分割性能的影响更大。这为未来的分割模型设计提供了重要的指导方向。

Abstract: Comparing transformer backbones for image segmentation is confounded: each is paired with a different decoder, recipe, and pretraining, so reported differences rarely reflect the backbone itself. We introduce the Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter (LUMA), a lightweight, backbone-agnostic mask-transformer head that treats any backbone as a black-box feature extractor, letting a set of queries read from its features through cheap cross-attention. LUMA matches the accuracy of EoMT, the state-of-the-art efficient ViT-segmenter, at lower cost, while attaching unchanged to isotropic, hierarchical, convolutional, and mixture-of-experts backbones alike. Holding this head fixed, we benchmark 20 backbones, 11 pretraining schemes and a range of resolutions on ADE20K and Cityscapes under one modern recipe. We find that ``efficient'' token mixers fail to deliver efficiency even at the high resolutions that motivate them, with plain ViT holding the throughput Pareto-front at every resolution. Additionally, the pretraining objective, not the architecture, the lever the field has tuned hardest, governs segmentation quality.

</details>


### [62] [NoPA: Non-Parametric Online 3D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00529)
*Qi Xun Yeo,Seungjun Lee,Yan Li,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NoPA提出了一种非参数分布对象表示方法，用于实时3D场景图生成，解决了现有高斯表示方法的几何细节损失和合并不准确问题，同时保持实时推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的3D场景图生成方法无法实时工作，因为环境映射计算成本高且需要中间点云表示。近期工作使用轻量级高斯分布替代点云，虽然加速了推理，但存在两个关键弱点：1）每个对象由单个3D高斯近似导致3D几何细节严重损失；2）近似与真实几何之间的差异加剧了在线推理中对象候选合并的不准确性。

Method: 1）提出NoPA，将每个对象表示为独立的非参数分布，保留3D几何信息同时保持实时推理；2）提出定制化合并策略，利用核密度估计上的最大均值差异实现鲁棒的对象候选合并；3）维护固定粒子集；4）通过亲和力高的对象间关系传播来纠正误分类对象导致的关系损失。

Result: 实验显示NoPA在保持实时推理速度的同时，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: NoPA通过非参数分布表示对象，解决了现有方法的几何细节损失和合并不准确问题，实现了既保持几何信息又实时推理的3D场景图生成。

Abstract: Classic 3D scene graph generation approaches fail to work in real-time due to the heavy computational cost of environment mapping and the need to generate intermediate point-cloud representations. To alleviate this issue, a recent work eschews point clouds in favor of a lightweight Gaussian distribution for each object. This approximation drastically speeds up inference and enables real-time 3D scene graph generation. However, the representation has two key weaknesses. \textbf{1)} Each object is approximated by a single 3D Gaussian, which causes a severe loss of 3D geometric detail. \textbf{2)} The discrepancy between this approximation and the true object geometry exacerbates the inaccurate merging of object candidates during online inference. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{NoPA}, which represents each object as a separate non-parametric distribution. This formulation retains 3D geometric information while preserving real-time inference of the parametric Gaussian formulation. To build upon our novel object representation, we propose a tailored merging strategy to recover coherent object instances. Specifically, we leverage maximum mean discrepancy on kernel density estimates to enable robust merging of object candidates during online exploration while minimizing added computational complexity. The key is to maintain a fixed particle set per object. Furthermore, to rectify the relation loss caused by misclassified objects, NoPA propagates relationships between objects with high affinity. Experiments show that NoPA substantially outperforms current methods without sacrificing real-time inference speed.

</details>


### [63] [Creating Impactful Autonomous Driving Datasets: A Strategic Guide from Research Gap to Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00710)
*Richard Schwarzkopf,Jonas Merkert,Frank Bieder,Annika Bätz,Alexander Blumberg,Carlos Fernandez,Felix Hauser,Fabian Immel,Christian Kinzig,Hendrik Königshof,Fabian Konstantinidis,Martin Lauer,Willi Poh,Nils Rack,Kevin Rösch,Yinzhe Shen,Marlon Steiner,Gleb Stepanov,Dominik Strutz,Ömer Şahin Taş,Julian Truetsch,Kaiwen Wang,Royden Wagner,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个战略性自动驾驶数据集设计框架，强调通过诊断数据问题或评估问题来最小化资源投入，并以KITScenes数据集为例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶研究主要关注数据集的内容而非设计策略，这对于资源有限的小型实验室和初创公司尤为不利。他们需要一个战略性的数据集设计框架来有效利用稀缺资源。

Method: 提出一个诊断-选择框架：首先诊断研究问题是数据问题还是评估问题，然后选择能够填补差距的最小数据操作符，只有在没有更便宜的替代方案时才记录新数据。通过分析主要自动驾驶数据集的演化验证该框架。

Result: 开发了一个包含缺口识别、操作符选择、传感器套件设计和标注策略的战略性框架，并基于该框架构建了KITScenes数据集系列作为运行案例研究。

Conclusion: 战略性数据集设计应该从问题诊断开始，通过选择最小必要的数据操作符来填补研究缺口，这能帮助资源有限的团队更有效地创建有影响力的自动驾驶数据集。

Abstract: Well-designed autonomous driving datasets have fundamentally shaped research progress, yet existing literature primarily describes what datasets contain rather than how to strategically design impactful ones. This is especially limiting for small and medium-sized labs and startups that cannot afford to misallocate scarce resources. We argue that impactful dataset creation begins with a diagnosis: whether a research question is blocked by a data problem or an evaluation problem, and proceeds by selecting the minimal data operator(s) that closes the resulting gap, recording new data only when no cheaper operator(s) suffices. We analyze the evolution of major autonomous driving (AD) datasets through this lens and distill a strategic framework spanning gap identification, operator choice, sensor suite design, and annotation strategy. We ground the framework in a running case study of our KITScenes dataset family. The datasets are available at: https://kitscenes.com/

</details>


### [64] [GEAR-Seg: A Grounded Explainable Agent for Reasoning Segmentation and Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00544)
*Yanan Wang,Wen Li,Yibin Ying,Zhenghao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GEAR-Seg（基于地标的可解释推理分割代理），通过将视觉像素转换为密集的、属性丰富的文本来解耦感知和推理，实现显式的、可追踪的逻辑链，并在多个推理分割基准上获得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的端到端推理分割模型通常将感知和推理纠缠在一个不透明的黑盒中，严重限制了可解释性和可扩展性。需要一种更透明、可追踪的方法来处理复杂的隐式查询。

Method: 提出GEAR-Seg框架，通过解耦类无关分割、语义描述和大型语言模型（LLM）推理三个模块，将视觉像素转换为密集的、属性丰富的文本表示。这是一个零样本推理框架，将隐式推理转化为显式的、可追踪的逻辑链。

Result: 在多样的推理和细粒度参考分割基准上获得了高度竞争性的性能。利用该框架构建了GEAR-131K大规模基准（超过3.8万张图像，65.6万问答-掩码对），引入了为复杂现实世界操作导向推理设计的多方面分类法。蒸馏实验显示，仅使用自动化管道监督的轻量模型能够接近成本高昂的人工标注基准的上限性能。

Conclusion: GEAR-Seg通过显式解耦感知和推理，实现了可解释、可扩展的推理分割框架，不仅性能优越，还能作为高效的数据引擎，为未来研究提供了重要的基准和自动化标注能力。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation requires localizing targets based on complex, implicit queries. Current end-to-end models typically entangle perception and deduction into an opaque black box, severely limiting interpretability and scalability. To address this, we propose GEAR-Seg (Grounded Explainable Agent for Reasoning Segmentation), an explicitly decoupled agent that shifts the paradigm by translating visual pixels into dense, attribute-rich text. By decoupling class-agnostic segmentation, semantic description, and Large Language Model (LLM) deduction, GEAR-Seg transforms implicit reasoning into an explicit, trackable logic chain. As a zero-shot inference framework, it achieves highly competitive performance across diverse reasoning and fine-grained referring segmentation benchmarks. Furthermore, GEAR-Seg inherently functions as a highly scalable data engine. Utilizing this engine, we construct GEAR-131K, a massive benchmark (over 38k images, 656k QA-mask pairs) introducing a multifaceted taxonomy tailored for complex real-world manipulation-oriented reasoning. Finally, distillation experiments demonstrate that lightweight models supervised exclusively by our automated pipeline closely match the upper-bound performance of costly human-annotated baselines.

</details>


### [65] [ECoSim: Data Efficient Fine-Tuning for Controllable Traffic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00545)
*Yu-Hsiang Chen,Wei-Jer Chang,Yi-Ting Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级的控制适应框架，为预训练的扩散和自回归交通模型添加多模态可控性（草图、潜在行为代码和文本），仅需少量配对控制数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控交通仿真对自动驾驶系统测试至关重要，但现有方法通常需要大量标注数据来重新训练大型生成模型

Method: 通过身份初始化的FiLM层调制中间特征，在不重新训练基模型的情况下添加新的控制模态，采用上下文感知的条件转移机制

Result: 在Waymo Open Sim Agents Challenge上评估，展示了强大的可控性（使用少于1%的配对控制数据），支持反事实场景生成和长尾合成，同时保持稳定的闭环驾驶真实性和安全性

Conclusion: 该框架为可控交通仿真开辟了新可能性，通过预训练生成模型的轻量级适应实现有针对性的场景生成

Abstract: Controllable traffic simulation is critical for testing autonomous driving systems, yet existing approaches often require retraining large generative models with extensive annotated data. We introduce a lightweight control adaptation framework that enables multi-modal controllability (sketch, latent behavior codes, and text) for pretrained state-of-the-art diffusion and autoregressive traffic models. By modulating intermediate features through identity-initialized FiLM layers, our method efficiently adds new control modalities while preserving the base model's generative prior. Evaluated on Waymo Open Sim Agents Challenge, our approach demonstrates strong controllability with less than 1% of the paired control data. Through context-aware condition transfer, our framework enables counterfactual scenario generation and long-tail synthesis while maintaining stable closed-loop driving realism and safety. Our framework unlocks new possibilities for controllable traffic simulation, enabling targeted scenario generation through lightweight adaptation of pretrained generative models. Project page: https://ecosim-web.github.io/

</details>


### [66] [Partial Skeleton Visibility for Action Recognition: A Constrained Field-of-View Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00716)
*Yingjie Dai,Tianyang Xu,Yanglin Deng,Xiao-Jun Wu,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PartialVisGraph：一种用于受限视野下鲁棒骨架动作识别的新型超图框架，通过引入可学习虚拟超边和单头样本自适应Transformer，显著提升了部分可见性场景下的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界部署中（如第一人称视觉、拥挤监控、可穿戴设备或边缘机器人），有限的视野范围常导致显著的关节点可见性丢失，而现有模型大多假设完整干净的骨架输入，无法有效处理部分可见性问题

Method: 1. 构建高度表达力的超图，引入可学习虚拟超边形成软关联矩阵，捕获超越传统成对图的高阶依赖关系；2. 提出单头样本自适应Transformer，自适应地将关节点特征聚合到超边上，同时显式地融入可见性先验；3. 建立严格的评估协议，在NTU RGB+D 60和120数据集上创建逼真的视野模拟基准

Result: PartialVisGraph在部分可见性条件下始终达到最先进的准确率，在严重视野限制的子集上相比最近强基线提升高达68.8%，同时在完整可见性设置下也保持优异性能

Conclusion: 该研究为无约束环境中可部署的基于骨架的动作理解提供了一条原则性和实用性的路径，有效解决了实际部署中的部分可见性问题

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition has achieved remarkable success by exploiting joint coordinates and their topological connections, yet prevailing methods overwhelmingly assume complete and clean skeleton inputs. In real-world deployments, such as egocentric vision, crowded surveillance, wearable devices, or edge robotics, limited field-of-view (FoV) frequently causes substantial joint visibility dropout, leading to severe performance degradation that existing models are largely unprepared to handle. To bridge this critical yet underexplored gap, we introduce PartialVisGraph, a novel hypergraph framework tailored for robust skeleton action recognition under constrained FoV. We first construct highly expressive hypergraphs by introducing learnable virtual hyperedges that form a soft incidence matrix, capturing flexible high-order dependencies beyond conventional pairwise graphs. We then propose the Single-Head Sample-Adaptive Transformer, which adaptively aggregates joint features onto hyperedges while explicitly incorporating a visibility prior. This prior selectively gates information flow, preventing occluded or out-of-view joints from corrupting reliable feature propagation. We further establish rigorous evaluation protocols with realistic FoV simulation benchmarks on NTU RGB+D 60 and 120. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PartialVisGraph consistently achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under partial visibility, with gains of up to 68.8\% on subsets with severe FoV restrictions compared to recent strong baselines, while remaining superior on full-visibility settings. Our approach offers a principled and practical pathway toward deployable skeleton-based action understanding in unconstrained environments.

</details>


### [67] [ConRTF: Edge-Constrained Boundary Distribution Refinement for Realtime TransFormer Table Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00734)
*Eliott Thomas,Tri-Cong Pham,Mickael Coustaty,Aurelie Joseph,Gaspar Deloin,Vincent Poulain d'Andecy,Jean-Marc Ogier,Antoine Doucet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为EFL（边缘约束细粒度定位损失）的损失函数，用于表格结构识别任务，通过在训练中编码表格特定的几何先验来提升边界定位精度，同时保持实时推理速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于检测的表格结构识别方法将表格元素视为通用对象，忽略了表格布局的基本特性：行和列在结构上扮演不同角色，它们的边界具有不同的重要性。小误差在行或列边界中会传播导致错误的单元格分配和结构不一致。

Method: 提出了Edge-constrained Fine-grained Localization loss (EFL)损失函数，将表格特定的几何先验编码到训练目标中：行类元素强调其水平边界监督，列类元素优先考虑垂直边界。EFL在训练期间指导边界细化，无需改变推理流程。方法结合了分布式边界细化(D-FINE)的实时检测器，整个方法称为ConRTF。

Result: 在PubTables-1M和两个私有数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比优化基线和多个实时检测器（包括RT-DETRv2和YOLOv10-11）都有一致的改进，在同等推理速度下GriTS分数提升最高达+1.6个点。方法还具有数据效率，仅使用2k-3k标注表格就能保持稳健的准确性。

Conclusion: EFL损失函数通过编码表格结构的几何不对称性，有效提升了表格结构识别的边界定位精度，同时保持了实时推理速度，提供了一种既高效又数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Table Structure Recognition (TSR) aims to recover the row and column layout of tables from document images, a key step in document understanding pipelines. Accurate TSR depends on precise boundary localization: small errors in row or column boundaries can propagate into incorrect cell assignments and structural inconsistencies. Yet detection-based approaches treat table elements as generic objects, ignoring a fundamental property of table layout: rows and columns play structurally distinct roles and their boundaries carry unequal importance. We propose an Edge-constrained Fine-grained Localization loss (EFL) that formalizes this structural asymmetry by encoding table-specific geometric priors into the training objective: row-like elements are supervised with emphasis on their horizontal boundaries, while column-like elements prioritize vertical boundaries. Implemented within a real-time detector with distribution-based boundary refinement (D-FINE), EFL operates during training only and guides boundary refinement toward structurally meaningful adjustments with no change to the inference pipeline. The proposed approach, ConRTF, is also data-efficient, maintaining robust accuracy with as few as 2k--3k annotated tables. Experiments on PubTables-1M and two private datasets show consistent improvements over the optimized baseline and several real-time detectors including RT-DETRv2 and YOLOv10-11, with gains of up to +1.6 GriTS points at equal inference speed.

</details>


### [68] [BrainFIBRE: A Foundation Model via Information Decomposition for Brain Microstructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00573)
*Zijian Dong,Yi Lin,Ji Fang,Jianxiong Zhou,Kwun Kei Ng,Juan Helen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainFIBRE是首个大脑微结构基础模型，通过自监督部分信息分解技术从NODDI衍生图像中学习可解释的微结构表征，在多种预测任务中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散MRI对早期脑血管和神经退行性变化特别敏感，NODDI能生成三种可生物物理解释的微结构图像。然而，标准表征学习方法难以解耦每个图像中的独特信息与它们之间的共享和协同交互，整合这些3D图像具有挑战性。

Method: 提出了BrainFIBRE模型：1) 基于55,592名UK Biobank参与者的NODDI衍生图像进行预训练；2) 提出自监督部分信息分解(SPID)，首次将PID引导的多模态学习扩展到自监督领域；3) 采用新颖的反事实候选构建(CCC)范式，通过模态丢弃和交换来扰动模态间对齐；4) 使用专家混合架构在没有下游标签的情况下解耦独特、协同和冗余信息。

Result: 在白种人和亚洲人群队列中，BrainFIBRE在预测年龄、性别、脑血管和神经退行标志物以及认知功能的多样化任务中均达到最先进性能。模型产生了神经生物学可解释的表征，揭示了任务和队列特定的交互模式。

Conclusion: BrainFIBRE为神经影像在微结构层面建立了多功能基础，实现了可转移的微结构表征学习，能够捕捉生物物理解释的独特、协同和冗余信息交互。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI probes brain microstructure with particular sensitivity to early cerebrovascular and neurodegenerative changes. Neurite Orientation Dispersion and Density Imaging (NODDI) decomposes the diffusion signal into three biophysically interpretable maps: neurite density index (NDI), orientation dispersion index (ODI), and free water fraction (FWF), capturing neurite packing, fiber coherence, and extracellular fluid. These 3D maps offer a rich substrate for transferable microstructural representations, yet integrating them is challenging: standard representation learning struggles to disentangle the unique information in each map from their shared and synergistic interactions. We present BrainFIBRE, the first foundation model for brain microstructure, pretrained on NODDI-derived maps from 55,592 UK Biobank participants. We propose Self-supervised Partial Information Decomposition (SPID), which extends PID-guided multimodal learning to the self-supervised regime for the first time. A novel Counterfactual Candidate Construction (CCC) paradigm perturbs inter-modality alignment through modality dropping and swapping, providing the contrastive signal for a Mixture-of-Experts architecture to disentangle unique, synergistic, and redundant information without any downstream label. On both Caucasian and Asian cohorts, BrainFIBRE achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse tasks predicting age, sex, cerebrovascular and neurodegenerative markers, and cognition, while yielding neurobiologically interpretable representations that reveal task- and cohort-specific interaction patterns. BrainFIBRE establishes a versatile foundation for neuroimaging analysis at the microstructural level.

</details>


### [69] [Prototype Memory-Guided Training-Free Anomaly Classification and Localization in Prenatal Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00744)
*Huanwen Liang,Yuhao Huang,Xiliang Zhu,Yuanji Zhang,Xuedong Deng,Xinru Gao,Guowei Tao,Yuhan Zhang,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的产前超声异常分类与定位框架，只需每类少量参考图像，实现多类异常检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前异常分类定位对胎儿健康至关重要，但超声诊断面临挑战：异常低流行率、高度异质性，且现有深度学习方法需要大规模标注数据，少样本学习需要针对新类别微调，在资源有限的临床环境中实用性受限

Method: 提出免训练框架，包含三个关键组件：1）包含多粒度原型的内存库，显式建模类级语义和异常特征；2）原型驱动的软合并机制，聚合判别特征检测异常区域；3）类感知细化策略，利用原型一致性改进类别预测

Result: 在多中心产前超声数据集（1,149个病例，2,357张图像，9个类别）上进行广泛验证，提出的方法优于竞争对手方法

Conclusion: 提出首个免训练的产前超声异常分类定位框架，仅需少量参考图像，解决了数据稀缺和资源有限临床环境中的实际挑战，在产前超声异常检测中表现出优越性能

Abstract: Prenatal anomaly classification and localization is of critical importance for fetal health and pregnancy management. Although ultrasound (US) is the primary modality for prenatal screening, accurate diagnosis remains challenging due to the low prevalence and high heterogeneity of anomalies. Existing deep learning methods for prenatal tasks rely on large-scale annotated datasets, which are difficult to obtain in practice. Although few-shot learning alleviates data scarcity, it typically requires fine-tuning for new categories, limiting its practicality in resource-limited clinical settings. To address these challenges, we propose a training-free framework for multi-class prenatal US anomaly classification and localization that operates with only a few reference images per class, representing the first exploration of this setting. Our framework comprises three key components: (1) a memory bank with multi-granular prototypes that explicitly models both class-level semantics and anomaly characteristics; (2) a prototype-driven soft merging mechanism that aggregates discriminative features to detect the anomaly region; and (3) a class-aware refinement strategy that leverages prototype consistency to improve category prediction. Extensively validated on a multi-center prenatal US dataset containing 1,149 cases, with a total of 2,357 images and 9 categories, our proposed method outperforms the competitors.

</details>


### [70] [Caption Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00578)
*Seref Baris Cagliyan,Umut Ozdemir,Merve Tapli,Emre Akbas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaBM（Caption Bottleneck Models）是一种新的解释性模型框架，它用自由形式自然语言生成的图像描述替代传统的固定概念集，通过纯文本分类器实现无信息泄漏的瓶颈架构，并能够自主发现高质量数据集特定概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）存在几个关键问题：需要昂贵的专家标注或依赖LLM生成的固定概念集；"开放词汇"变体通常使用静态概念集，限制了新概念的发现并引入标签偏差；存在信息泄漏问题，未建模的视觉特征绕过瓶颈影响解释的完整性。

Method: CaBM框架包含三个核心组件：1）使用LMM（大型多模态模型）为图像生成自由形式的自然语言描述；2）训练一个仅基于这些文本描述的纯文本分类器，从架构上保证无信息泄漏；3）训练后分析文本分类器，自主发现高质量、数据集特定的概念集。

Result: 在细粒度和粗粒度基准测试中，CaBM实现了与现有方法竞争的准确率，同时在不需要外部词典或手动标注的情况下保持了模型的可解释性，消除了预定义概念集的约束。

Conclusion: CaBM通过将图像表示为自然语言描述并训练纯文本分类器，成功克服了传统概念瓶颈模型的局限性，提供了一种自动发现数据集特定概念的无泄漏可解释架构，为可解释AI提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide interpretability by routing predictions through a layer of human-understandable concepts. However, defining an optimal concept set for a specific dataset remains an open challenge. Existing approaches rely on expensive expert annotations or LLM-generated lists based solely on class names. Even "open-vocabulary" variants typically depend on static concept sets, which restrict discovery and introduce label bias. Furthermore, traditional CBMs often suffer from information leakage, where unmodeled visual features bypass the bottleneck and compromise the integrity of the explanations. To overcome these limitations, we propose Caption Bottleneck Models (CaBM), a framework that circumvents the need for predefined concept sets by replacing rigid concept layers with free-form natural language. By representing images via LMM-generated captions and training a classifier strictly on this text, CaBM ensures a leakage-free architecture by construction. Additionally, by analyzing the text classifier post-training, CaBM autonomously discovers high-quality, dataset-specific concepts. Our results across fine- and coarse-grained benchmarks demonstrate that CaBM achieves competitive accuracy while preserving interpretability without the constraints of external dictionaries or manual labeling.

</details>


### [71] [GaussianFusion: Unified 3D Gaussian Representation for Multi-Modal Fusion Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00746)
*Xiao Zhao,Chang Liu,Mingxu Zhu,Zheyuan Zhang,Linna Song,Qingliang Luo,Chufan Guo,Kuifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GaussianFusion，一个基于3D高斯表示的多模态融合新框架，替代传统BEV表示，在连续3D空间统一多传感器特征，有效保留细节并支持多种3D感知任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鸟瞰图（BEV）表示使用离散网格，导致显著细节损失，限制特征对齐和跨模态信息交互。多模态融合感知需要更有效的表示方法来保持细节并改善特征融合。

Method: 提出基于3D高斯表示的多模态融合通用框架：1) 设计前向投影的多模态高斯初始化模块；2) 设计共享跨模态高斯编码器，基于注意力机制迭代更新高斯属性；3) 在共享连续3D高斯空间中统一多模态特征。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上：1) 3D目标检测性能超过BEVFusion基线2.6 NDS；2) 3D语义占据预测变体超过GaussFormer 1.55 mIoU，仅使用30%的高斯点，速度提升450%。

Conclusion: GaussianFusion是一个任务无关的通用多模态融合框架，3D高斯表示能自然统一多模态特征，有效保留细节，在多种3D感知任务中表现出优异的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The bird's-eye view (BEV) representation enables multi-sensor features to be fused within a unified space, serving as the primary approach for achieving comprehensive 3D perception. However, the discrete grid representation of BEV leads to significant detail loss and limits feature alignment and cross-modal information interaction in multimodal fusion perception. In this work, we break from the conventional BEV paradigm and propose a new universal framework for multi-modal fusion based on 3D Gaussian representation. This approach naturally unifies multi-modal features within a shared and continuous 3D Gaussian space, effectively preserving edge and fine texture details. To achieve this, we design a novel forward-projection-based multi-modal Gaussian initialization module and a shared cross-modal Gaussian encoder that iteratively updates Gaussian properties based on an attention mechanism. GaussianFusion is inherently a task-agnostic model, with its unified Gaussian representation naturally supporting various 3D perception tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate the generality and robustness of GaussianFusion. On the nuScenes dataset, it outperforms the 3D object detection baseline BEVFusion by 2.6 NDS. Its variant surpasses GaussFormer on 3D semantic occupancy with 1.55 mIoU improvement while using only 30% of the Gaussians and achieving a 450% speedup.

</details>


### [72] [EPO: Boosting 3D Foundation Models with Edge-based Pose Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00579)
*Mattia D'Urso,Christian Sormann,Mattia Rossi,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EPO是一个无需特征跟踪的位姿优化框架，通过边缘图对齐提升3D基础模型的重建质量，在保持快速推理优势的同时达到或超过传统Bundle Adjustment方法的精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D基础模型通过绕过传统特征提取和匹配实现了快速推理，但其几何精度低于传统方法。虽然可以通过类似Bundle Adjustment的后处理来改进，但这需要提取特征轨迹，从而丧失了原有的速度优势。

Method: 提出完全可微分的Edge-based Pose Optimization框架，使用边缘图对齐作为几何优化的代理，完全避免了特征提取和轨迹构建。该方法通过优化相机位姿使边缘图在多个视图之间对齐。

Result: 在多个数据集和任务上的广泛评估表明，EPO在精度上匹配或超越了类似Bundle Adjustment的方法，同时显著降低了运行时间和内存需求。其较低的内存占用使其适合消费级硬件。

Conclusion: EPO为3D基础模型提供了一种高效的几何优化解决方案，既保持了快速推理的优势，又提升了重建质量，特别适合资源受限的环境。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{Edge-based Pose Optimization (EPO)}, a trackless geometric optimization framework specifically designed to boost the Structure-from-Motion reconstructions generated by 3D Foundation Models. These models achieve rapid inference by bypassing the time-consuming feature extraction and matching stages of traditional pipelines, where explicit correspondences between each 3D point and multiple images, referred to as tracks, are established.
